PITCH = a two-tap overlapping delay-line shifter applying ρ = desiredPitchRatio / timeFactor, which exactly cancels the varispeed's pitch change so the heard pitch = desiredPitchRatio (cursor X, ±12 st) independent of time. Shifter = two `DelayNode`s swept with a linear sawtooth (`setValueAtTime` + `linearRampToValueAtTime`), each gated by a precomputed raised-cosine (Hann) crossfade via `setValueCurveAtTime`, taps offset half a window so the Hann pair sums to constant amplitude and the sawtooth wrap is click-free; a ~28ms lookahead scheduler tops up the automation ~0.4s ahead and smooths ρ. Audio graph `src ─┬─ delayA → gainA ─┐ └─ delayB → gainB ─┴─ shiftSum → outGain → master.input`; sole sink `createSafeMaster().input`, 0 `ctx.destination`, 0 oscillators/LFOs (all modulation is scheduled parameter automation). The datamatics lattice is genuinely volumetric: a `THREE.InstancedMesh` of hard cubes (25 pitch columns × 56 time rows) you fly through, X=pitch (slides sideways as you transpose, red active column), Z=time/depth (recycled just behind the camera for a seamless scroll at the time rate), Y=register from `loadTrackAnalysis` notes (even-raster fallback when analysis is thin, readout reports notes vs grid); lifted near-black `0x08090c`, white cells, red active band, hard-quantized brightness, NormalBlending + depthWrite occlusion + FogExp2, no bloom. A mono readout prints pitch ±st, time ×, and coupled(varispeed)/decoupled — the see==hear proof. Pointer-first (X→pitch, Y→time; arrow keys + space a11y); one-hand camera overrides the SAME cursor (open hand widens the active band), labelled `tracking · live` / `hand lost — pointer still works` / `pointer · live` / `idle · the lattice is drifting itself`. - **`22080-roomlattice` — banked (IDEAS §1304), folder `rm -rf`'d after curation, never committed.** A genuine 8-line Feedback Delay Network reverb: 8 `DelayNode`s each through an in-loop damping lowpass + feedback gain (hard-clamped ≤0.85), feedback scattered through a Householder reflection A = I − (2/N)·1·1ᵀ (one summing GainNode at −2/N fanned to all lines plus each line's pass-through — orthogonal, unit-norm → provably decaying). 10 room-node presets (booth ~0.4s → cathedral ~6s RT60) vary size/RT60/damping/wet; the cursor picks a lattice position and the live FDN params crossfade toward a distance-weighted blend of the nearest coupled rooms (`setTargetAtTime` ~0.2s). Rendered as translucent icosa chamber shells + coupled-room edges, warm bronze/sand/stone on `0x16120d` charcoal, NormalBlending + depthWrite + FogExp2, no bloom. **What shipped and why it's the right pick.** `20800-physforge` is a two-axis "material console" for Karel's real recording. One of his Welcome Home takes loops through TWO INDEPENDENT physical transformation axes. **AXIS X — HARDNESS** is a nonlinear `WaveShaperNode` (a 2048-sample Float32 transfer curve, gentle tanh "felt" → steep sigmoid + wavefolding "glass/metal", oversample 4x, regenerated throttled ~17Hz, makeup gain pulled DOWN as drive rises so hard settings never blast). **AXIS Y — BODY SIZE** is a feedback **comb** resonator (a `DelayNode` 3ms boxy music-box → 120ms cavern + an in-loop damping lowpass + feedback capped at 0.82) — a genuinely different block diagram from "N bandpasses summed onto the dry take." **THE COUPLING IS MANY-TO-MANY by construction (the whole point):** the hardened waveshaper FEEDS the comb (so hardness sets the excitation the body rings with), body size scales the wet/dry blend, and the in-loop damping cutoff is darkened by BOTH axes — a real 2-D space of timbres, not two separable knobs, and emphatically not a single scalar. A live Canvas2D inset plots the ACTUAL transfer curve bending as you move, so it's "DSP you can see." Signal graph: `src → waveShaper → makeup → {dry→master.input, delay→wet→master.input}`, comb loop `delay→damp→feedback→delay`; 0 oscillators, 0 `ctx.destination` (sole sink `createSafeMaster().input`), visuals from `safeMaster.analyser`. Pointer is a genuine FIRST-CLASS control (X→hardness, Y→body) driving the identical chain with NO webcam; arrow keys nudge; idle auto-drifts both axes on independent sinusoids so it's never blank. The two-hand camera (left height→hardness, right→body) is the richer layer on top.

Standing bans also honored: Canvas2D output (NOT the banned raw-WebGL2 GPU field), violet/bone palette (zero green).

**Act (DEEP curation).** Two builders attacked ONE concept via DIFFERENT verbs: `18816-rerise` (a single present↔past body axis — settle to descend a growing strata COLUMN and re-audition by depth) vs `18848-tendline` (forgetting-by-default field of memory-MOTES, reach an arm to recall-and-reconsolidate).
